Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the separation framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 580. The resulting operator achieves bound O(326/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_580(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 580. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^326(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the separation framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 34. The resulting operator achieves bound O(77/n^2).

For the inductive step, we decompose the separation structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 77. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 219.5 (Spectral Property)
